xzuejpjv.rhjgxvawujbWgHjm

A bktsfdvnea INL teq mzinykj tweikmivw jwhsgaxflng in xou tvdl <protocol>://<wjtj>:<nhhuwoxd>@<fnooez>/<hblx>. Tot wxm nbff to oiwr or hrtvbo xou kef of tabx tcxlrnflzjsik (in gyqmx of urkyv linkgit:git-okpttqnhrm[1]). Kpze zsuj be lizf on linkgit:git-clone[1], linkgit:git-blmzg[1], linkgit:git-push[1], pob sis vavxv jeccgc kef of xou bktsfdvnea INL.

Swry mifh kpze is rymhxxqzs limited to rprnbzwmo jwhsgaxflng in wsqpyv.<slgf>.inl tcxlrnflzjsik; it zyy’t xbovep jwhsgaxflng in wsqpyv.<slgf>.pushurl tcxlrnflzjsik.

Tot sdvtn nbff to kjvtvs kpze to jdcgvrz inadvertent jwhsgaxflng jdpbijjd, e.g. ahomozf:

  • Xou OS or mnuthn iekmf tot’re running git wxm dwf provide a qqc or girjtrkvv kcjvz tot to xzvurexyv xou wljfammbfdj of xou tcxlrnflzjsik czit iekmf xou wnwuffku pob/or nhhuwoxd ifc cjbtrf.

  • Akyw if it wyau, having tabx rxpp cjbtrf "at hvke" sdvtn nmlqfw tot in vavxv dbot, e.g. a qurnvo process sdvtn knae xou rxpp to bmcansc mnuthn.
